Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly With respect to the need to open Parliamental research under Articles 104, and 105, of our Constitution's 98th, Grand National Assembly of Turkey, in accordance with the Articles 104, and 105, in order to determine the measures that should be taken by investigating teacher appointing and the problems related to paid teaching in Turkey. [[Ankara]] [[Denizli]] [[İzmir]] [[Mersin]] [[& & & & &gt; &gt; ……]] [[Antalya]] [[İstanbul]] [[Kayseri]] [[Scholarship]] [[Adana]] [[Aydın]] [[Gaziantep]] [[Ankara]] [[Sakarya]] 15) Fahrettin Oğuz Tor [[♪ Heromarash ♪]] [[Inconceived]] [[Afyonkarahisar]] [[İstanbul]] [[Mughle]] [[Ankara]] [[Erzurum]] The number of teachers waiting to be appointed to the staff is increasing day by day in our country. According to the number of teachers waiting for the appointment, teachers cannot be appointed due to lack of staff assigned by the Finance Ministry. According to the numerical data of the 2015 BAUT exam, the number of teachers taking the test was 283,583. However, there are major teachers who do not take the S.E.A.B.T. exam. Therefore, the number of teachers applying for the education sciences exam is 428,459. If the number of graduates continues to increase at this rate each year, the number of teachers waiting to be appointed will triple in three years. THE number of teachers of the CSI is not really enough. Because many teachers who have lost hope and feel that they can no longer become teachers in public do not apply for this test. They work in different jobs to sustain their lives because no other skills have been created for unregistered teachers. Some work in the private sector, some in the public office, some in the police, some in the military. The lives of unregistered teachers grieve their consciences. There are people in the private sector who have cancers due to grief, suicides in depression, construction, restaurants, and hotels. In addition, those who want to do the right teaching job after graduating from the university are crushed by the gears of the wheel that spins in the private sector. The government's inconsistencies have upset the psychology of teachers who are unable to appoint conflicting statements from ministers and prime minister. Over the past year, the number of unsupervised teachers has reached approximately 40. The number of paid teachers increases every year. The number of paid teachers announced this year is approximately 80,000. The teacher's deficit is being resolved with a paid teacher. Payday teachers are appointed regardless of which department graduates and whether they are in formation or not. It is not clear to us what logic they are assigned to pay teachers in seasonal labor logic. The number of education schools has increased in the past 14 years to 94. Each year, the number of graduates from education schools and formation training is 90 thousand. Although the number of graduate teachers is so high, the government is increasing the number of education schools every day. The plan, the unscheduled steps, unfortunately, have had bad consequences. For reasons revealed above, a parliamentary investigation is required to be conducted in Turkey to investigate the appointment of teachers and the questions regarding the paid teaching. Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly The Central Future Programme (OVP) and Central Timed Financial Plan (OVMP) are not published on the same day, and the Central Time Financial Plan is seen to be published on the same day. In addition, deep differences arise between the targets and predictions of the OVP (OVP) prepared three months apart for the three-year period between 2016-2018.
